Gold supporting: objective : to assess effectiveness of 5 - aminolevulinic acid ( 5 - ala, gliolan ( ® ) ) in patients treated for malignant glioma under typical daily practice conditions in spain, using complete resection rate ( cr ) and progression free survival at 6 months ( pfs6 ).
LLM supporting:  the drug gliolan, in the context of its use in malignant glioma surgery, is associated with the term 5 - aminolevulinic acid ( 5 - ala ). this association is based on the visiona study, which demonstrated that 5 - ala, when used in surgical procedures, was linked to higher complete resection rates and improved progression - free survival in patients with grade iv gliomas. the study included a retrospective review of data from 18 neurosurgery departments in spain, comparing outcomes between 5 - ala users and non - users. the results showed significant differences in favor of 5 - ala, with higher complete resection rates and better survival outcomes. 5 - aminolevulinic acid is also known to be a precursor in the heme biosynthesis pathway, which is crucial for the synthesis of hemoglobin and other heme - containing proteins, although this role is not directly related to its use in cancer therapy. the term'gliolan'is not an alternative name for 5 - ala but is used in the context of the investigational prodrug givosiran, which is not yet approved for treating malignant gliomas. therefore, the accurate term for the drug used in this context is 5 - aminolevulinic acid ( 5 - ala ).
